\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{80},

(47)

and that the step size
\mu>0
satisfies
\mu\leq\frac{c_{2}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
In addition, assume that the conclusions of Lemma
13
hold
and that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\delta;8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{3}}{\kappa},

(48)

where
\delta=\delta_{4r+1}
denotes the Restricted Isometry Property of rank
4r+1
.
Then it holds that

\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\sqrt{2}-1}}{40}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

Under the assumption
that this a priori estimate holds,
the next lemma
shows that the quantity

\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

can be bounded from above
by the quantity

\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}
.
The proof of this lemma has been deferred to Section
E.3
.

Lemma 19
.

Let
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
and assume that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{1600},

(49)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{3\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)}\cdot\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{40}.

(50)

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}}{5}.

(51)

Moreover, it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq 3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.

(52)

The following key lemma allows us to control

\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}
iteratively.
Its proof can be found in Section
E.4
.

Lemma 20
.

For sufficiently small absolute constants

c_{1},c_{2},c_{3},c_{4},c_{5},c_{6}>0
the following statement holds.
Let
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
and assume that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{1},

(53)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|},

(54)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(55)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(56)

Moreover, assume that the step size satisfies
\mu\leq\frac{c_{4}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
Furthermore, assume that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds
and that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(57)

\displaystyle\max\left\{\delta;8\sqrt{\frac{2rd}{m}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{6}}{\kappa},

(58)

where
\delta=\delta_{4r+2}
denotes the Restricted Isometry Constant of rank
4r+2
.
Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|.

C.2.3
Lemmas controlling the distance between
\mathbf{X}_{\star}
and
\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

In the following, let
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
denote any matrix norm, which satisfies the inequality

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{X}\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Z}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{Y}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|

(59)

for all matrices
\mathbf{X}
,
\mathbf{Y}
, and
\mathbf{Z}

with dimensions such that the matrix product
\mathbf{X}\mathbf{Y}\mathbf{Z}
is well-defined.
Note that all Schatten-
p
norms
have this property.
In particular, this includes the spectral norm
\big\|\cdot\big\|

and the Frobenius norm
\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}
.

In the following, we are interested
in establishing upper bounds for

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
,
where either
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}
or
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|
.
Instead of estimating these quantities directly,
we will instead derive upper bounds for the quantity

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

(60)

To be able to relate this quantity with

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

one can then use the following lemma.

Lemma 21
.

Let
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
be a norm for which inequality (
59
) holds.
Assume that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}.

(61)

Then the following inequalities hold:

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|,

(62)

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(1+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

(63)

A comparable lemma was proven in
(
Stöger and Soltanolkotabi 2021
)

in a more general setting but with less explicit constants.
For the sake of completeness, we included in Appendix
F.1
.

The following lemma allows us to control the quantity

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
iteratively.
We note that
a similar lemma has already been proven in
(
Stöger and Soltanolkotabi 2021
)

in a more general setting with less explicit constants.
For the sake of completeness, we again included a proof in Appendix
F.2
.

Lemma 22
.

Let
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
be a norm
which is submultiplicative in the sense of inequality (
59
).
Assume that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2},

(64)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|},

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{48},

(65)

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{48}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(66)

and that the step size satisfies

\mu\leq\frac{1}{1024\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
Then it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+5\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

Given an upper bound for
\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

we can obtain an estimate for
\text{dist}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)

by using the following technical lemma.

Lemma 23

(Lemma 5.4 in
(
Tu et al. 2016
)
)
.

Let
\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
be two matrices
and assume that
\text{rank}(\mathbf{U})=\min\left\{r;d\right\}
.
Then it holds that

\displaystyle\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2(\sqrt{2}-1)\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{U})}\big\|\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}-\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\big\|_{F}^{2},

where
\text{dist}\left(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V}\right)
is defined in (
6
).

To check the prerequisite of the Davis-Kahan inequality (Lemma
26
) in our proof,
we will also need the following auxiliary lemma,
which provides us with an a priori bound for

\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\big\|
.
Its proof can be found in Appendix
F.3
.

Lemma 24
.

There are absolute constants
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3}>0
such that the following holds.
Assume that
\mu\leq\frac{c_{1}}{\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}

and

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|},

(67)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(68)

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(69)

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

C.2.4
Statement and proof of the main convergence lemma

We now have all the ingredients in place to prove the main lemma in this section,
which is stated below.

Lemma 25
.

There are absolute constants
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3},c_{4}>0

chosen sufficiently small
such that the following statement holds.
Assume that the spectral initialization
\mathbf{U}_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|\leq c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

(71)

and that for every
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
we have that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq c_{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(72)

Moreover, we assume that the conclusion of Lemma
13

holds for

T=\Big\lceil\frac{8}{\mu\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}\log\left(16r\right)\Big\rceil.

Furthermore, we assume that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\delta;8\sqrt{\frac{2rd}{m}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{3}}{\kappa},

(73)

where
\delta=\delta_{4r+2}
denotes the Restricted Isometry Property of order
4r+2
.
In addition, assume that
\mu\leq\frac{c_{4}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
Then for every iteration
t
with
0\leq t\leq T
it holds that

\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\leq r\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{2t}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|.

(74)

In particular, we have that

\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{T},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\leq\frac{1}{16}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(75)

where
\mathbf{U}_{\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
denotes a matrix which satisfies
\mathbf{U}_{\star}\mathbf{U}_{\star}^{\top}=\mathbf{X}_{\star}
.

Proof of Lemma
25
.

We prove by induction that for all iterations
t
with
0\leq t\leq T
the following inequalities hold:

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{16}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)^{t}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F},

(76)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\leq

\displaystyle c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(77)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}c_{1},

(78)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\leq

\displaystyle 3c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(79)

and, for every
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle c_{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(80)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle 3c_{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(81)

The constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
are the same as in assumptions (
71
)
and (
72
)
and are thus, in particular, independent of the iteration number
t
.

First, we check that these inequalities hold for
t=0
.
Inequality (
76
) is immediate.
Inequalities (
77
) and (
79
) follow from assumption (
71
).
Inequalities (
80
) and (
81
) are due to assumption (
72
).
It remains to establish inequality (
78
) for
t=0
.
Using the Davis-Kahan inequality (see Lemma
26
)
and assumption (
71
) it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{0}}\big\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\right)\big\|}{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}\leq\sqrt{2}c_{1}.

This shows that the above inequalities hold for
t=0
.

For the induction step, assume now that these inequalities hold for some
t
.
First, we observe that it follows from the induction assumption (
79
) and Weyl’s inequalities that

\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
for
c_{1}<1/3
.
Moreover, note that since we assumed that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds
we obtain from Proposition
14
that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

(82)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(16\sqrt{\frac{2rd}{m}}+2\delta\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+4\left(\delta+4\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4c_{3}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{6c_{3}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{10c_{3}}{\kappa}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(83)

where inequality
(a)
follows from assumption (
73
).
Inequality
(b)
is due to the induction hypotheses (
79
) and (
81
) with
c_{1}\leq 1/3
and
c_{2}\leq 1/3
.
Next, we note that from Lemma
22
applied with

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}
it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+5\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+5\mu\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+15\mu\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\frac{15\mu c_{3}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{16}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.

Inequality
(a)
follows from the Restricted Isometry Property combined
with Lemma
8
.
Inequality
(b)
is due to Lemma
21
and
inequality (
78
).
Inequality
(c)
follows from assumption (
73
)
and inequality
(d)
is due to the fact we can choose
c_{3}\leq\frac{1}{240}
.
Thus, using the induction assumption,
we see that inequality (
76
) holds for
t+1
.

Next, our goal is to prove inequality (
77
) for
t+1
.
For that, we note that it follows from Lemma
22

with
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|
that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|

(84)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+5\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

(85)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{8}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)c_{1}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+50c_{3}\mu\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

(86)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(87)

where inequality
(a)
follows from the induction hypothesis (
77
) and inequality (
83
).
Inequality
(b)
holds
since we can choose
c_{1}
and
c_{3}
in such a way that
c_{3}\leq\frac{c_{1}}{400}
.
This proves inequality (
77
) for
t+1
.

We observe that Lemma
24
yields the
a-priori bound

\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\big\|\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

Thus, we can apply the Davis-Kahan inequality
(see Lemma
26
)
which together with
inequality (
87
)
yields that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t+1}}\big\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|}{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}\leq\sqrt{2}c_{1}.

(88)

This proves inequality (
78
) for
t+1
.
Next, we apply Lemma
21

and (
87
) to obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(1+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t+1}}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq 3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)\big\|\leq 3c_{1}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

which proves inequality (
79
) for
t+1
.

Next, we can apply Lemma
20
since all assumptions are satisfied
and it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

(89)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

(90)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)c_{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)+3c_{1}\mu\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

(91)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle c_{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(92)

Inequality
(a)
is due to inequalities (
79
) and (
80
).
Inequality
(b)
holds since we can choose that
c_{1}\leq\frac{c_{2}}{48}
.
This proves inequality (
80
).

Next, we want to prove inequality (
81
) for
t+1
.
First, we apply Lemma
18
and we obtain
for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
the a-priori bound

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\sqrt{2}-1}}{40}\cdot\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

This allows us to apply Lemma
19

and we obtain for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
the sharper bound

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq 3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}\overset{\eqref{ineq:intern786}}{\leq}3c_{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

which shows inequality (
81
) for
t+1
.
This completes the induction step.

To complete the proof of Lemma
25

it remains to prove inequalities (
74
) and (
75
).
For that, we first observe that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{t}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\sqrt{2r}\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{t}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|.

Inequality
(a)
follows from Lemma
21

with
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}

which is applicable since we have shown by induction that (
78
) holds for
0\leq t\leq T
.
Inequality
(b)
holds since we have proven (
76
) for all
0\leq t\leq T
.
Inequality
(c)
holds since
\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}
has rank at most
2r
.
Thus, we can apply Lemma
23
and obtain that

\displaystyle\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 18r\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{2t}\cdot\frac{\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9c_{1}r}{\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)}\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{2t}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|,

where in the last inequality, we have used assumption (
71
).
This proves inequality (
74
)
since
c_{1}\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}-1}{9}
.
Next, we note that for
t=T
, the above inequality yields that

\displaystyle\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{T},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{9c^{2}_{1}r}{\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)}\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{2T}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{9c^{2}_{1}r}{\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)}\exp\left(\frac{-T\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}.

In inequality
(a)
, we have used again assumption (
71
).
Inequality
(b)
is due to the elementary inequality
\ln(1+x)\leq x
for
-1<x
and the assumption
\mu<\frac{c_{4}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
for sufficiently small
c_{4}>0
.
Inequality
(c)
follows from

T=\Big\lceil\frac{8}{\mu\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}\log\left(16r\right)\Big\rceil

(and from the fact that we can choose
c_{1}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\sqrt{2}-1}}{3}
).
This proves inequality (
75
).
Thus, the proof of Lemma
25
is complete.
∎

C.3
Proof of Theorem
2

Now we have all the ingredients in place to prove the main result of this paper,
Theorem
2
.

Proof of Theorem
2
.

In the following

c>0
denotes a sufficiently small absolute constant.
First, by Lemma
6
we know that due to our assumption
m\gtrsim rd\kappa^{2}
, with probability
1-\exp(-d)

the measurement operator
\mathcal{A}
satisfies the Restricted Isometry Property
of order
6r
with a constant
\delta=\delta_{6r}\leq\frac{c}{\kappa}
,
where
c>0
is a sufficiently small absolute constant.

Set

T:=\bigg\lceil\frac{8}{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}\log\left(16r\right)\bigg\rceil.

Note that since
r\geq 1
and the assumption
\mu\leq\frac{c_{1}}{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}
for small
c_{1}>0
, we have
T\geq 1
.
Let
\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
be an
\varepsilon
-net of the unit sphere in
\mathbb{R}^{d}

with
\varepsilon=1/2

such that
|\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}|\leq 6^{d}
. Now note that
2T\leq 6^{d}
,
where we have used the assumption

\mu\geq\frac{32}{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})6^{d}}\log\left(16r\right)
.
Thus, it follows from Lemma
13

that
with probability at least
1-2\exp(-10d)

it holds that

|\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\rangle|\\
\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\big\|_{2}

for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}

and for all
0\leq t\leq T
.
Next, we know from Lemma
16

and due to our assumption
m\gtrsim rd\kappa^{2}

that
with probability at least
1-5\exp(-d)
, the inequalities

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right),

(93)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

hold for a sufficiently small constant
c>0
.
Thus, all the assumptions of Lemma
25

are fulfilled.
It follows that

\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\leq r\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)^{2t}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|

(94)

for all
0\leq t\leq T

and

\text{dist}\left(\mathbf{U}_{T},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}.

(95)

Due to inequality (
95
) and since
\delta_{6r}<1/10

we can apply Lemma
17

which yields that for
t\geq T
,

\displaystyle\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right)\leq\left(1-c\mu\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)^{t-T}\text{dist}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{T},\mathbf{U}_{\star}\right).

(96)

Thus, by combining (
93
),
(
94
), and (
96
)
we obtain the conclusion of Theorem
2
.∎

Appendix D
Proof for the Spectral Initialization
(Proof of Lemma
16
)

The Davis-Kahan
\sin\theta
-theorem
(
Davis and Kahan 1970
)
states that the eigenspaces of a symmetric matrix
are stable under perturbations of that matrix.
Among others, we will need this result in order to show that
the spectral initialization recovers the eigenspace of the ground truth matrix sufficiently well.
We also will need it in order to show that
\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}
is sufficiently close to
\mathbf{U}_{0}
.

To state this theorem,
recall that for a symmetric matrix
\mathbf{Z}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}

with eigendecomposition
\mathbf{Z}=\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{Z}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{\mathbf{Z}}\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}

the matrix
\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{Z},r}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
consists of the first
r
columns of
\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{Z}}

and the matrix
\mathbf{U}_{\mathbf{Z},r,\bot}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times(n-r)}
consists of the remaining
n-r
columns.
Moreover, recall that the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{Z}
are ordered
such that their magnitude is decreasing,
i.e.,
|\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{Z})|\geq|\lambda_{2}(\mathbf{Z})|\geq\ldots\geq|\lambda_{n}(\mathbf{Z})|
.

Lemma 26

(Davis-Kahan inequality, Corollary 2.8 in
(
Chen et al. 2021
)
)
.

Set
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|

or
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|=\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}
.
Let
\mathbf{Z}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
and
\mathbf{Z}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
be two symmetric matrices,
such that the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{Z}_{1}
satisfy

|\lambda_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|>|\lambda_{r+1}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\right)|
for an integer
1\leq r<d
.
Let the eigendecompositions of
\mathbf{Z}_{1}
and
\mathbf{Z}_{2}
be given by

\mathbf{Z}_{1}=\mathbf{U}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{\top}
, respectively
\mathbf{Z}_{2}=\mathbf{U}_{2}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{\top}
.
Then, if the assumption

\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|\leq\left(1-1/\sqrt{2}\right)\left(|\lambda_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|-|\lambda_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|\right)

is fulfilled,
it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{U}_{2,r,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{1,r}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\mathbf{U}_{1,r}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|}{|\lambda_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|-|\lambda_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})|}.

(97)

Proof of Lemma
16
.

(1) We write

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{X}_{\star}=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{A}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(98)

Let
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}}
be any
\varepsilon
-net on
S^{d-1}
with
\varepsilon=\frac{1}{2}
of size at most
6^{d}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\leq

\displaystyle 2\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}}}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathbf{x}^{\top}\left(\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{A}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{x}

(99)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}}}\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\star}\mathbf{x}\right).

(100)

For each
i\in[m]
,
we have that
\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{x}\right]=\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\star}\mathbf{x}
.
Moreover,
the inner product
\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle
is a centered Gaussian random variable with variance
\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}^{2}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{x}
is a centered Gaussian random variable with variance
1
.
Thus, for each fixed
\mathbf{x}
,

\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\star}\mathbf{x}\right)

is a sum of
m
independent and centered sub-exponential random variables with subexponential norm bounded by
K\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}
,
where
K
is an absolute constant (see
(
Vershynin 2018
, Lemma 2.7.7)
).
Therefore, by Bernstein’s inequality (see, for example,
(
Vershynin 2018
, Theorem 2.8.1)
),
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\star}\mathbf{x}\right)\right|\geq t\right)\leq\exp\left(-C^{\prime}\min\left\{\frac{mt^{2}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}^{2}},\frac{mt}{\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}}\right\}\right),

(101)

where
C^{\prime}>0
is some absolute constant.
Taking
t=\frac{1}{8}C\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right)
and a union bound over all points
\mathbf{x}
on
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\leq\frac{1}{4}C\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|_{F}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{r}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right)

(102)

with probability at least
1-\exp(d\log(6)-C^{\prime}C^{2}d)\geq 1-\exp(-4d)
for some sufficiently large constant
C>0
.

We assume that (
102
) holds and that
m>C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
.
Then Weyl’s inequalities imply that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}((\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))>\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),\quad|\lambda_{r+1}((\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))|<\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(103)

Since
\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{r}
is a diagonal matrix with entries
\lambda_{1}((\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})),\dots,\lambda_{r}((\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))
,
it follows from the definition of
\mathbf{U}_{0}=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{r}^{1/2}

that
\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}
is the best rank-
r
approximation of
(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})
. Consequently, we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|+\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|

(104)

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|+\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\leq C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}},

(105)

where in the second inequality, we used the Eckart-Young-Mirsky theorem.

(2) Due to Lemma
12
we have

\displaystyle(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})=(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))-\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}.

(106)

It follows that

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\|\leq\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))\|+|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\rangle|.

(107)

For a fixed
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
,
we obtain with an analogous argument as for (
102
) that with probability at least
1-\exp(-4d)
,

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))\|\leq C\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|_{F}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{r}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right).

(108)

The second term in (
107
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\mathbf{A}_{i}\rangle\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\rangle.

(109)

Here,
\sum_{i=1}^{m}\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\mathbf{A}_{i}\rangle\langle\mathbf{A}_{i},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\rangle

is a sum of
m
independent sub-exponential random variables with mean zero due to the rotation invariance of the Gaussian measure.
Moreover, each term has sub-exponential norm
K\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|_{F}
.
Applying Bernstein’s inequality as in the proof of (
102
),
we obtain that for each fixed
\mathbf{w}
with probability at least
1-\exp(-4d)
,

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\rangle\leq\frac{1}{4}C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{r}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right).

(110)

Then, by taking a union bound over
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
,
it follows from (
107
)
that with probability at least
1-\exp(-2d)

that for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\leq\frac{1}{2}C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{r}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}+\frac{d}{m}\right).

(111)

We now assume that (
111
) holds and that
m>4C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
.
Then it follows from Weyl’s inequalities that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}((\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))>\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),\quad|\lambda_{r+1}((\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))|<\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(112)

It follows from the Eckart-Mirsky-Young theorem and the definition of
\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}

that
\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}
is the best rank-
r
approximation of
(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|+\big\|(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

(113)

\displaystyle\leq 2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\leq 2C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}.

(114)

This finishes the proof of inequality (
40
).
Finally, (
41
) follows from (
39
) and (
40
) via the triangle inequality.

(3) From (
106
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})-(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

(115)

\displaystyle=\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top})+\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}.

(116)

It follows from Lemma
6

that there exists an absolute constant
C_{1}>0

such that for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
m\geq\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\kappa^{2}rd
, with probability at least
1-\exp(-d)
,
the measurement operator
\mathcal{A}
satisfies the
Restricted Isometry Property of order
6r
with constant

\displaystyle\delta:=\delta_{6r}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\kappa}.

(117)

Then for any
\mathbf{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}
with orthonormal columns and for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
, when
m\geq\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\kappa^{2}rd
, with probability at least
1-2\exp(-d)
,

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\mathbf{V}\big\|_{F}

(118)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\mathbf{X}_{\star}\rangle|\big\|(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top})\mathbf{V}\big\|_{F}+|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))\rangle|\big\|\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\mathbf{V}\big\|_{F}

(119)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}))\rangle|

(120)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\alpha\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\frac{1}{2}C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}.

(121)

Here in (a) we use property (
10
) in Lemma
8
and the fact that
\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\mathbf{V}
is of rank 1,
and in (b) we use (
117
) and, moreover, (
110
) with a union bound over
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}
.

We now proceed under the assumption that the inequalities in parts (1) and (2) hold. We use the following notations for spectral initialization:

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}^{\top},\quad\mathbf{U}_{0}=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}{\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{r}}^{1/2},

(122)

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{w}}\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\mathbf{w}}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{w}}^{\top},\quad\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}{\widetilde{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}}^{1/2}.

(123)

Denote

\mathbf{Z}_{1}:=(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),\quad\mathbf{Z}_{2}:=(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

and

\mathbf{Z}_{1,r}:=\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top},\quad\mathbf{Z}_{2,r}:=\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}.

Recall the definition of
\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}
in (
122
) and (
19
).
We have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1,r}-\mathbf{Z}_{2,r}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\|_{F}

(124)

\displaystyle\leq\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}+\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}.

(125)

For the first term in (
125
), we have

\displaystyle\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

(126)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2,r})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

(127)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}+\|(\mathbf{Z}_{2}-\mathbf{Z}_{2,r})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

(128)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}+\|(\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}^{\top})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

(129)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}+\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

(130)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}+C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F},

(131)

where in the last inequality we used Weyl’s inequality and (
111
),
which implies

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})=|\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})|\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{2}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|\leq C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}.

(132)

From (
111
) and (
102
),
it follows that when
m\geq C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2}\|\leq\frac{3C}{2}\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}.

(133)

Similar to (
132
), using (
111
) and Weyl’s inequalities
we obtain that

\displaystyle|\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})-\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})|

\displaystyle\leq C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}},

(134)

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})

\displaystyle\leq C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}.

(135)

Therefore, if
m>16C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
,
the spectral gap between
\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})
and
\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{2})

can be bounded from below by

\displaystyle\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})\geq\left(1-2C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(136)

When
m\geq 51C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
,
we have from (
133
) and (
136
),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3C}{2}\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

(137)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\bigg)\left(1-2C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

(138)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\bigg)(\sigma_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})-\sigma_{r+1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})).

(139)

Thus, the prerequisites of Lemma
26
(Davis-Kahan inequality) are satisfied.
It follows that when
m\geq 51C^{2}\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w},\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}}{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}.

(140)

Hence,
when
m\geq\left(51C^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\kappa^{2}rd
,
we obtain from (
131
) and (
121
) that

\displaystyle\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+2\sqrt{2}C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\big\|_{F}

(141)

\displaystyle\leq 2\big\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\big\|_{F}\leq\left(2\alpha+C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(142)

For the second term in (
125
), we have when
m\geq\left(51C^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}

(143)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}

(144)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\|_{F}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}

(145)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\alpha+C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F},

(146)

where the last inequality is due to (
142
).

We now consider the second term in (
146
). Recall the definition of
\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}
in (
20
). We have for
m\geq\left(51C^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(147)

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\big\|\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(148)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{2}_{r,\mathbf{w}}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|}\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(149)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|}\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(150)

\displaystyle=\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(151)

\displaystyle=\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top})\big\|\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(152)

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\big\|_{F}

(153)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}3C\kappa\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\cdot\frac{2\sqrt{2}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2})\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\|_{F}}{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}

(154)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}6\sqrt{2}C\kappa\left(\alpha+\frac{1}{2}C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(155)

where (a) is due to (
41
) and (
140
), and (b) is due to (
121
). Therefore from (
146
) and (
155
), we obtain for
m\geq\left(51C^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\|\left(\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{r,\perp}\|_{F}

(156)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\alpha+C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+6\sqrt{2}C\kappa\left(\alpha+\frac{1}{2}C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

(157)

From (
142
), (
156
), and (
125
), we conclude that if
m\geq\left(51C^{2}+\frac{C_{1}}{\alpha^{2}}\right)\kappa^{2}rd
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{0}\mathbf{U}_{0}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{0,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq\left(2\alpha+C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\left(2\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+3\sqrt{2}C\kappa\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right).

(158)

This finishes the proof of (
42
).
∎

Appendix E
Proofs of lemmas concerning the distance
between the virtual sequences and the original sequence

E.1
Some auxiliary estimates

In order to prove Lemma
18
and Lemma
20

we will need several auxiliary estimates.
These are summarized in the following lemma.

Lemma 27
.

Assume that the measurement operator
\mathcal{A}

has the Restricted Isometry Property with constant
\delta=\delta_{4r+1}\leq 1
.
Moreover, assume that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds.
Then, the following inequalities hold.

1.

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

(159)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F},

(160)

2.

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\leq 2\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F},

(161)

3.

\begin{split}\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\leq\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|,\end{split}

(162)

4.

and

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+\left(2\delta+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

(163)

Proof of Lemma
27
.

To prove inequality (
160
), we compute that

\displaystyle(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)=

\displaystyle(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)+(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}})\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A})\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)+\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle-\langle\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\right),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},

where in equation
(a)
we used Lemma
12
.
It follows that

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}.

(164)

By using the triangle inequality, we obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}+\big\|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\big|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\right)\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{4\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{m}}\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\big\|_{2}+\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\big\|_{F}+\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+\left(\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Inequality
(a)
follows from the RIP-assumption combined with Lemma
8

and from the fact that
\big\|\mathbf{w}\big\|_{2}=1
.
Inequality
(b)
is a consequence of the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}
is a rank-one projection and of the triangle inequality.
In inequality
(c)
, we used that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds and Lemma
8
.
In inequality
(d)
, we used the RIP of rank
2r+1
.
Inequality
(e)
is due to the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}
is an orthogonal projection and due to the triangle inequality.
In inequality
(f)
, we used that
\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}
has rank at most
2r
.
This proves inequality (
160
).

To prove inequality (
161
) we compute first that

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right)-\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\big|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\right)\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\delta\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

In inequalities
(a)
and
(b)
we used Lemma
8
.
This proves inequality (
161
).

Next, we prove the third inequality.
For that, we observe that using Lemma
12
it holds that

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}.

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}+|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\rangle|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|+4\sqrt{\frac{d}{m}}\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|,

where inequality
(a)
holds due to Lemma
8
,
since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}
is a rank-one projection,
and since we assumed that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds.
Inequality
(b)
is again due to Lemma
8

and since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}
is an orthogonal projection.
This proves inequality (
162
).

It remains to prove inequality (
163
).
We note that it holds that

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)

(165)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)-\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right))\rangle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},

(166)

where in the last line we applied Lemma
12
.
It follows from the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\big|\langle\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\right),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\delta\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

(167)

\displaystyle+2\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

(168)

\displaystyle+\left(2\delta+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

In inequality
(a)
we applied Lemma
8

and that the conclusion of Lemma
13
holds.
This proves inequality (
163
).
Thus, the proof of Lemma
27
is complete.
∎

E.2
Proof of Lemma
18

Proof of Lemma
18
.

We define the shorthand notation

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}

\displaystyle:=\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right).

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t},

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}

\displaystyle=\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}.

We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t})-\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}+\mu\underset{=:(i)}{\underbrace{\mathbf{M}_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})}}+\mu\underset{=:(ii)}{\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}}}

\displaystyle+\mu\underset{=:(iii)}{\underbrace{(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{M}_{t}}}+\mu\underset{=:(iv)}{\underbrace{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)}}

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\underset{=:(v)}{\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)}}.

We want to estimate the spectral norm of these terms individually.
Before that, we note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

(169)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(170)

Inequality
(a)
follows from the triangle inequality and
inequality
(b)
follows from assumption (
45
).
Inequality
(c)
is a consequence of assumption (
46
).
Moreover, we note that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}=\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right).

It follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

(171)

\displaystyle\leq
