\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}+\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(3\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}+1\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{2c_{3}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(\frac{4c_{3}}{\kappa}+1\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F},

(172)

where in inequality
(a)
we used inequalities (
160
) and (
161
) from Lemma
27
.
Inequality (b) is due to assumption (
48
).
Note that it also follows from these estimates that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\frac{2c_{3}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(\frac{4c_{3}}{\kappa}+1\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}3\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

(173)

where inequality
(a)
follows from (
172
).
Inequality
(b)
is a consequence of the
assumptions (
46
)
and (
47
)
(and by choosing the absolute constant
c_{3}>0
small enough).

Now we are in a position to estimate the spectral norms of the terms
(i)
-
(v)
.

Estimating term (i):

We compute that that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ineq:weakboundintern5}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+c_{1}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Estimating term (ii):

We compute that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F},

where in the last inequality we used assumptions (
44
)
and (
46
).

Estimating term (iii):

With the same argument as for term
(i)
we observe that

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+c_{1}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Estimating term (iv):

With the same argument as for term
(ii)
we compute that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\big\|_{F}\leq 3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}.

Estimating term (v):

First, we compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{t}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}_{t}+\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right).

It follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+15\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}.

For inequality
(a)
we used the assumptions (
44
) and
(
47
).
Inequality
(b)
is a consequence of inequalities (
170
) and (
173
).

Conclusion:

By summing up all terms we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+2\mu\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+c_{1}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+6\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\left(4\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+15\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mathbf{M}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1+2\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+2c_{1}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+12\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})c_{3}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+6\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left(\frac{4c_{3}}{\kappa}+1\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+4\mu^{2}\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+30c_{3}\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+60c_{3}\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+15\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1+2\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\left(2c_{1}+24c_{3}\right)\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+6\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+4\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+60c_{3}\mu^{2}\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+\left(12c_{3}\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+30c_{3}\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\sqrt{2}-1}}{40}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

Inequality
(a)
follows from inequality (
172
).
Inequality
(b)
is due to assumptions (
46
), (
47
),
and the assumption
\mu\leq\frac{c_{2}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}

for a sufficiently small absolute constant
c_{2}>0
.
This completes the proof of Lemma
18
.
∎

E.3
Proof of Lemma
19

Proof of Lemma
19
.

Let
\mathbf{R}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
be an orthogonal matrix.
We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\right)^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}=\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\right)^{\top}-(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R})\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}

(174)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(2\sqrt{\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

(175)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(2\sqrt{\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

(176)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{20}\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}.

(177)

In inequality
(a)
we used Assumption (
49
).
By choosing the orthogonal matrix
\mathbf{R}
as the minimizer of Procruste’s problem,
i.e., such that
\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}
is minimal,
we obtain by Lemma
23
that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}}{\sqrt{2\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\right)}}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}}{\sqrt{\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}}\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}}{20}.

Inequality
(a)
follows from Assumption (
49
) and Weyl’s inequalities for singular values.
For inequality
(b)
we used Assumption (
50
).
Inequality (
177
) combined with this inequality chain yields that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}}{10}\cdot\frac{\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}}{\sqrt{\left(\sqrt{2}-1\right)\cdot\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}}{5}.

(178)

In order to proceed we note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\frac{1}{5}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

In inequality
(a)
we have used inequality (
178
).
By rearranging terms we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\frac{1}{5}}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.

This shows inequality (
52
).
Then (
51
) follows directly from inserting the above inequality into (
178
).
∎

E.4
Proof of Lemma
20

The key idea in the proof of Lemma
20

is to decompose

\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)

into a sum of the form

\begin{split}&\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\\
=&\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(1+\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(1+\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\\
&+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Delta}.\end{split}

(179)

The first summand can be interpreted as a contraction mapping applied to the matrix

\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

and thus can be expected to have a smaller Frobenius norm than

\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\|_{F}
.
In contrast, the term
\mathbf{\Delta}
,
which will be determined explicitly in the proof of Lemma
20
,
can be interpreted as an additive error term
which, as we will show, has relatively small Frobenius norm.

To deal with the first summand we need the following auxiliary lemma.

Lemma 28
.

Denote by
\lambda_{\max}(\mathbf{A})
the largest eigenvalue of a symmetric matrix
\mathbf{A}

and by
\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{A})
the smallest eigenvalue of
\mathbf{A}
.
Assume that the assumptions of Lemma
20
are satisfied.
Then it holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 0,

(180)

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{2},

(181)

\displaystyle\big\|\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{128}.

(182)

Proof of Lemma
28
.

Note that the assumptions
\mu\leq\frac{c_{4}}{\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
, (
54
), and (
56
)
together with Weyl’s inequalities imply

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left((\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top})-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|-\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|-\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 0.

for sufficiently small
c_{2},c_{3},c_{4}>0
.
This shows inequality (
180
).

We observe that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)+\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}\left(-\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)+\left(c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)+\left(c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)^{2}\lambda_{\min}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)+\left(c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{2}.

(183)

Inequality
(a)
follows from Weyl’s inequalities.
Inequality
(b)
follows from assumption (
55
) and (
56
).
For inequality
(c)
we used assumptions (
53
), (
55
) for suffciently small
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3}
,
and Weyl’s inequalities.
This proves inequality (
181
).

To prove inequality (
182
),
we first establish an upper bound for the largest eigenvalue of

\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}
.
For that let
\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be arbitrary.
We use the orthogonal decomposition
\mathbf{x}=\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}+\mathbf{x}_{\perp}
,
where
\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}
is the orthogonal projection of
\mathbf{x}
onto the column span of
\mathbf{X}_{\star}
.
We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}-\mathbf{x}_{\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}_{\bot}-2\mathbf{x}_{\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ineq:interndistance3}}{\leq}

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}^{2}-2\mathbf{x}_{\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}.

(184)

Next, we observe that

\displaystyle-\mathbf{x}_{\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left(2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}}{16}.

In the last inequality we have used the assumptions (
55
) and (
56
) for sufficiently small
c_{2},c_{3}>0
.
Combining this estimate with (
184
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{x}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\left(\frac{\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}}{8}-\frac{\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\parallel}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{\perp}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{128}\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{x}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{128}.

This implies that

\lambda_{\max}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right)\leq 1+\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{128}.

(185)

This inequality, together with inequality (
180
), yields inequality (
182
).
Thus, the proof of Lemma
28
is complete.
∎

With Lemma
28
in place,
we can show that the first term in the decomposition (
179
)
indeed has a smaller Frobenius norm than the term

\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)
.

Lemma 29
.

Assume that the assumptions of Lemma
20
are satisfied.
Then, it holds that

\begin{split}&\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\big\|_{F}\\
&\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
29
.

We first compute that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}\big\|\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{128}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F},

(186)

where in the last line we used inequality (
182
) from Lemma
28
.
In order to proceed, we consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle\underset{=:\mathbf{N}_{1}}{\underbrace{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle-\mu\underset{=:\mathbf{N}_{2}}{\underbrace{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}}}

\displaystyle-\mu\underset{=:\mathbf{N}_{3}}{\underbrace{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}}}.

We estimate the Frobenius norm of the three terms individually.
For the first term we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{N}_{1}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\big\|\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\left(1+\mu\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{2}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F},

where in inequality
(a)
we have used (
180
) and in
(b)
we have used inequality (
181
)
from Lemma
28
.
The Frobenius norm of the term
\mathbf{N}_{2}
can be estimated by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{N}_{2}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left(\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left[2\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)+\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2c_{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F},

where we have used Assumptions (
55
)
and (
56
).
With similar arguments, we can estimate the Frobenius norm of the term
\mathbf{N}_{3}
by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{N}_{3}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}.

By using Lemma
19
we obtain that

\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{3\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}}{5}.

It follows that

\big\|\mathbf{N}_{3}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{3\left(2c_{2}+c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}}{5}.

By summing up our estimates for
\big\|\mathbf{N}_{1}\big\|_{F}
,
\big\|\mathbf{N}_{2}\big\|_{F}
, and
\big\|\mathbf{N}_{3}\big\|_{F}

and choosing the constants
c_{1},c_{2}>0
small enough
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{4}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.

Inserting this estimate into (
186
) yields that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{128}\right)\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{4}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F},

where in the last line, we used our assumption on the step size
\mu
.
This completes the proof of Lemma
29
.
∎

With the auxiliary estimates in Lemma
29

we can give a proof of Lemma
20
.

Proof of Lemma
20
.

First, we compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)+\mu^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mu\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right].

Analogously, we can compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)+\mu^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle+\mu\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right].

Thus, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}

(187)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{1}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{2}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{3}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{4}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{4}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{5}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{6}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{7},

(188)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{1}:=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top})\right)

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{2}:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{3}:=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top},

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{4}:=

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top},

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{5}:=

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle-\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{6}:=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{7}:=

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]

\displaystyle-\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right].

Recall that Lemma
29
shows that

\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\big\|_{F}\leq\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}.

To complete the proof, we need to derive upper bounds for
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{i}\big\|_{F}
, where
i=2,3,\ldots,7
.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{2}\big\|_{F}
:

We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{2}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)+\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right).

Thus, we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{2}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

In the last inequality we used assumptions (
54
), (
55
),
and (
56
) for sufficiently small
c_{2},c_{3}>0
.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{3}\big\|_{F}
:

Since
\mathbf{M}_{3}=\mathbf{M}_{2}^{\top}
it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{3}\big\|_{F}\leq 5\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{4}\big\|_{F}
:

We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{4}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\ \mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right).

Again, using the assumptions (
54
) and (
56
),
and the triangle inequality we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{4}\big\|_{F}\leq 20\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\ \mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{5}\big\|_{F}
:

We compute

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{5}=

\displaystyle\underset{=:\mathbf{O}_{1}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle+\mu\underset{=:\mathbf{O}_{2}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{O}_{3}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{O}_{4}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)}}.

(189)

We estimate the Frobenius norm of these summands individually.
For the first term we observe that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{O}_{1}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\left(1+\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F},

where in inequality
(a)
we have used assumptions (
54
), (
56
),
and the assumption on the step size
\mu
.
In inequality
(b)
we have used assumption (
57
).

Using again assumptions (
54
), (
56
), and (
57
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{O}_{2}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle 3c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

For the term
\big\|\mathbf{O}_{3}\big\|_{F}
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{O}_{3}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\left(1+\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\left(1+\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+4\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|.

Inequality
(a)
follows from the assumptions (
54
) and (
56
),
and the assumption on the step size
\mu
.
In inequality
(b)
we used the estimate (
160
) from Lemma
27
.

For the term
\big\|\mathbf{O}_{4}\big\|_{F}
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{O}_{4}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(1+\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 6\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Inequality
(a)
follows from assumptions (
55
) and (
56
),
and the assumption on the step size
\mu
.
Inequality
(b)
is due to inequality (
161
) in Lemma
27
.
By summing up all terms we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{5}\big\|_{F}\leq\big\|\mathbf{O}_{1}\big\|_{F}+\mu\big\|\mathbf{O}_{2}\big\|_{F}+\big\|\mathbf{O}_{3}\big\|_{F}+\big\|\mathbf{O}_{4}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+3\mu c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+4\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+4\left(\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+6\delta\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\left(\left(2+3\mu\right)c_{5}+6\kappa\delta\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+4\left(\delta+\frac{4\sqrt{2d}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\right]\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+4\left(\delta+\frac{8\sqrt{rd}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\left(\left(2+3\mu\right)c_{5}+6c_{6}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+8c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+8c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{100}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}+8c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{3\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{100}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+8c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|,

where in inequality
(a)
we used the assumption (
58
).
Inequality
(b)
follows from choosing the constants
c_{5}
and
c_{6}
small enough.
To obtain inequality
(c)
we applied Lemma
19
.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{6}\big\|_{F}
:

Since
\mathbf{M}_{6}=\mathbf{M}_{5}^{\top}
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{6}\big\|_{F}\leq\frac{3\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{100}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+8c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|.

Estimating
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{7}\big\|_{F}
:

To deal with the term
\mathbf{M}_{7}
we first compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{7}=

\displaystyle\underset{=:\mathbf{L}_{1}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{L}_{2}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{L}_{3}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{L}_{4}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]}}

\displaystyle+\underset{=:\mathbf{L}_{5}}{\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]}}.

We estimate the Frobenius norm of the summands individually.
For
\big\|\mathbf{L}_{1}\big\|_{F}
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{1}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{5}^{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F},

where we have used assumption (
57
).
Next, we note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{2}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3c_{5}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3c_{5}\delta\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3c_{5}c_{6}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

Inequality
(a)
follows from
assumptions (
54
), (
56
), and (
57
).
Inequality
(b)
is due to Lemma
8

and inequality
(c)
is due to assumption (
58
).
In order to estimate
\big\|\mathbf{L}_{3}\big\|_{F}
we note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{3}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\left(\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right)\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right)\left(2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+c_{3}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\left(c_{5}+\delta c_{3}\right)\delta\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3c_{6}\left(c_{5}+\delta c_{3}\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}.

In inequality
(a)
we used the assumptions (
54
), (
56
), (
57
),
and Lemma
8
.
Inequality
(b)
follows from assumption (
56
) and since the constant
c_{3}>0
is chosen small enough.
Inequality
(c)
is due to assumption (
58
).

Next, we can estimate
\big\|\mathbf{L}_{4}\big\|_{F}
by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{4}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\left(2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+c_{3}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(c_{5}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})+\delta\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 6c_{6}\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right).

In inequality
(a)
we used assumptions (
54
), (
56
), and
(
57
)
as well as Lemma
8
.
Inequality
(b)
uses assumption (
56
).
Inequality
(c)
follows from inequality (
162
) in Lemma
27
.
Inequality
(d)
is due to assumption (
58
).

The norm
\big\|\mathbf{L}_{5}\big\|_{F}
can be estimated by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{5}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}^{\top}\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}.

(190)

In inequality
(a)
we used the triangle inequality
and the assumptions (
54
), (
56
).
In order to proceed, we note first that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+\left(2\delta+4\sqrt{\frac{2d}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|+\frac{2c_{6}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{3c_{6}}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(c_{5}+\frac{2c_{2}c_{6}}{\kappa}+\frac{3c_{3}c_{6}}{\kappa}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}),

where in inequality
(a)
we used Lemma
27
.
Inequality
(b)
follows from the assumptions (
58
).
Inequality
(c)
is due to assumption (
55
), (
56
),
and (
57
).
Moreover, it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\mathbf{w}}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}}\big\|_{F}\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\delta+8\sqrt{\frac{rd}{m}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{2c_{6}}{\kappa}\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right).

Inequality
(a)
follows from inequality (
162
) in Lemma
27
.
Inequality
(b)
is due to assumption (
58
).
Inserting the last two inequality chains into inequality (
190
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{L}_{5}\big\|_{F}\leq 6c_{6}\left(c_{5}+\frac{2c_{2}c_{6}}{\kappa}+\frac{3c_{3}c_{6}}{\kappa}\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

By summing up all terms
\big\|\mathbf{L}_{i}\big\|_{F}
for
i=1,\ldots,5
it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{7}\big\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle c_{5}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+3c_{5}c_{6}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+3c_{6}\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+6c_{6}\left(c_{5}+c_{3}\delta\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle+6c_{6}\left(c_{5}+\frac{2c_{2}c_{6}}{\kappa}+\frac{3c_{3}c_{6}}{\kappa}\right)\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right),

where the last inequality holds since the absolute constants
c_{3},c_{5},c_{6}>0

are chosen small enough.

Using the decomposition (
188
), the triangle inequality,
combined with our estimates for
\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\big\|_{F}
and for
\big\|\mathbf{M}_{i}\big\|_{F}
,
where
2\leq i\leq 7
,
we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t+1,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+30\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{50}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+16\mu c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\left(\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+90\mu c_{4}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{50}\cdot\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+16\mu c_{6}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\frac{3\mu c_{4}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{\kappa}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\mu\left(16c_{6}+\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{16}\right)\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}\mathbf{U}_{t,\mathbf{w}}^{\top}\right)\big\|_{F}+\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|,

where inequality
(a)
is due to Lemma
19
and the assumption on the step size
\mu
.
Inequality
(b)
is obtained by choosing
c_{4}<1/2
,
and the last inequality is obtained by choosing
c_{6}<\frac{1}{32}
.
∎

Appendix F
Proof of the lemmas controlling the distance between
\mathbf{X}_{\star}
and
\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

(Lemma
21
, Lemma
22
, and Lemma
24
)

F.1
Proof of Lemma
21

Proof of Lemma
21
.

We first note that
