\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}.

Using the submultiplicativity property of the
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
-norm it follows that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\big\|\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)^{-1}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|}{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}})}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

Recall that

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{\min}\left(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=1-\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\big\|=1-\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\big\|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4},

where in the last inequality, we used assumption (
61
).
It follows that

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq 2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

This proves inequality (
62
).
To prove inequality (
63
) we note that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(1+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|,

where in the last inequality we used (
62
).
This completes the proof of Lemma
21
.
∎

F.2
Proof of Lemma
22

Proof of Lemma
22
.

We define the shorthand notation

\mathbf{M}_{t}:=\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)=\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\underset{=:\mathbf{E}_{t}}{\underbrace{\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}.

Thus, we have that

\mathbf{U}_{t+1}=\left(\textbf{Id}+\mu\mathbf{M}_{t}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}.

We compute that

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}-\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\mu\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle-\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\mu\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}-\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle-\mu^{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}-\mu^{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{=:(I)}{\underbrace{\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle+\mu\underset{=:(II)}{\underbrace{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}

\displaystyle-\underset{=:(III)}{\underbrace{\left(\mu^{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}+\mu^{2}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}\right)}}.

We estimate the spectral norm of these terms individually.

Estimating term
(I)
:

We obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\left(1-\mu\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t})\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\mu\left(\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}})\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\right)\right)^{2}\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{2}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\right)\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu}{4}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

Inequality
(a)
is due to the submultiplicativity of the
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
-norm.
In inequality
(b)
and equality
(c)
we used the assumptions
\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}

and
\mu\leq\frac{1}{1024\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
In inequality
(d)
we used assumption (
64
).
Inequality
(e)
follows from assumption (
65
),
which, due to Weyl’s inequality, implies

\sigma^{2}_{\min}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})
.

Estimating term
(II)
:

We note that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\left(\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}\big\|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\left(\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\left(1+\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 3\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

In inequality
(a)
we used the submultiplicativity
of the
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
-norm.
Inequality
(b)
follows from the assumption
\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}

and
\mu\leq\frac{1}{1024\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.
In inequality
(c)
, we used Lemma
21
.
In inequality
(d)
we used the assumption
\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star},\bot}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\big\|\leq\frac{1}{2}
.
Thus, by using the assumption
\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{48}
it follows that

\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(II)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{16}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

Estimating term
(III)
:

We first note that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\left[\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right]\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|,

(191)

where
(a)
follows from the triangle inequality and

(b)
follows from Lemma
21
.
Moreover, we have that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right).

(192)

Inequality
(a)
follows from assumptions (
65
) and (
66
).
Thus, we obtain for term
(III)
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(III)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{4}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+2\mu\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 16\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+4\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+2\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(16\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}+8\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle+\left(4\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|+2\mu^{2}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)}{16}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+5\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

In inequality
(a)
we used the assumption
\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|\leq\sqrt{2\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
,
Lemma
21
, and inequality (
192
).
Inequality
(b)
is due to inequalities (
191
).
In inequality
(c)
we used the assumption that

\mu\leq\frac{1}{1024\kappa\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
.

Conclusion:

By adding up all terms, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(I)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\mu\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(II)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|(III)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\mu\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})}{8}\right)\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}_{\star}}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|+5\mu\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{U}_{t}}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

This completes the proof.
∎

F.3
Proof of Lemma
24

Proof of Lemma
24
.

Analogously, as in the proof of Lemma
22

we define the shorthand notation

\mathbf{M}_{t}:=\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)=\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}+\underset{=:\mathbf{E}_{t}}{\underbrace{\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)}}.

We note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|\leq\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\big\|\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\big\|\leq(c_{2}+c_{3})\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

With an analogous computation as in the proof of Lemma
22
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}=

\displaystyle\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\left(\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)-\mu^{2}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}

\displaystyle-\mu\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{t}-\mu^{2}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{t}.

When
c_{1}\leq 1/2
,
we have
\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\leq 1

by assumption (
67
). It follows from the assumptions
\mu\leq\frac{c_{1}}{\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|}
, (
68
), and (
69
) that
for sufficiently small
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3}>0

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t+1}\mathbf{U}_{t+1}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|\big\|\textbf{Id}-\mu\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{4}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|

\displaystyle+2\mu\big\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}+\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{M}_{t}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{U}_{t}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}-\mathbf{U}_{t}\mathbf{U}_{t}^{\top}\big\|+4\mu^{2}c_{2}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|^{2}\sigma_{\min}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\star}\right)+4\mu c_{3}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle+2(c_{2}+c_{3})^{2}\mu^{2}\big\|\mathbf{X}_{\star}\big\|\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(c_{2}+4c_{1}^{2}c_{2}+4c_{1}c_{3}+2(c_{2}+c_{3})^{2}c_{1}^{2}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{X}_{\star}).

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix G
Proofs regarding the Restricted Isometry Property and its consequences

G.1
Proof of Lemma
6

As already mentioned in Section
2
,
there exist similar versions of Lemma
6
in the literature
(see, e.g.,
(
Candès and Plan 2011
)
),
which, however, do not specify the dependence of the number of samples
m

on the constant
\delta>0
.
It would be possible to trace the steps of the
\varepsilon
-net argument in
(
Candès and Plan 2011
)

and work out the
\delta
-dependence explicitly.
However, this would lead to an extra
\log(1/\delta)
-factor, which is unnecessary.
The reason is that as
\delta
is decreased,
a covering with smaller balls is required, leading to a larger
\varepsilon
-net.
This observation suggests a proof strategy based on generic chaining.
Indeed, we will use the following general theorem from
(
Krahmer et al. 2014
)
,
which is proven via the generic chaining technique.
To state it, we define the diameter of a set of matrices
\mathcal{B}

with respect to some norm
\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|
as

\displaystyle d_{\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|}(\mathcal{B})

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\mathbf{B}\in\mathcal{B}}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathbf{B}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|.

Moreover,
we will also need Talagrand’s functional
\gamma_{2}\left(\mathcal{B},\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\left|\cdot\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\right|\right)

(
Talagrand 2005
)
,
where for a precise definition, we refer to
(
Krahmer et al. 2014
)
.

Theorem 30

(Theorem 3.1 in
(
Krahmer et al. 2014
)
)
.

Let
\mathcal{B}
be a set of matrices,
and
\bm{\xi}
be a random Gaussian vector,
i.e.,
\bm{\xi}
has i.i.d. entries with distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.
Set

\displaystyle E

\displaystyle:=\gamma_{2}(\mathcal{B},\big\|\cdot\big\|)\left(\gamma_{2}\left(\mathcal{B},\big\|\cdot\big\|\right)+d_{\|\cdot\|_{F}}\left(\mathcal{B}\right)\right)+d_{\|\cdot\|_{F}}(\mathcal{B})d_{\|\cdot\|}(\mathcal{B}),

(193)

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle:=d_{\|\cdot\|}(\mathcal{B})\left(\gamma_{2}\left(\mathcal{B},\big\|\cdot\big\|\right)+d_{\|\cdot\|_{F}}(\mathcal{B})\right),\quad U:=d_{\|\cdot\|}^{2}(\mathcal{B}).

(194)

Then, for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{\mathbf{B}\in\mathcal{B}}\left|\big\|\mathbf{B}\bm{\xi}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big\|\mathbf{B}\bm{\xi}\big\|_{2}^{2}\right|>c_{1}E+t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-c_{2}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{V^{2}},\frac{t}{U}\right\}\right),

(195)

where
c_{1},c_{2}>0
denote absolute constants.

With this result in place, we can give a proof of Lemma
6
. This proof strategy has been used in
(
Krahmer et al. 2014
, Section A.3)
.

Proof of Lemma
6
.

Since
\mathcal{A}
is a linear operator
we can write
\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})=\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\bm{\xi}
,
where
\bm{\xi}
is a Gaussian random vector with independent entries of length
m\binom{d+1}{2}

and

\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\begin{bmatrix}\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})^{\top}&&&\\
&\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})^{\top}&&\\
&&\ddots&\\
&&&\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})^{\top}\end{bmatrix}

is an
m\times(m\binom{d+1}{2})
block-diagonal matrix.
Here,
\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})\in\mathbb{R}^{\binom{d+1}{2}}
is a vector indexed by

\{(i,j)\in[d]\times[d]:i\leq j\}
such that

\displaystyle\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})(i,j)=\begin{cases}\sqrt{2}\mathbf{X}_{ij}&i\not=j\\
\mathbf{X}_{ii}&i=j.\end{cases}

(196)

Let

D_{r}:=\{\mathbf{X}\in\mathcal{S}^{d}:\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}=1,\ \mathrm{rank}(\mathbf{X})\leq r\}.

Then it follows from the identity
\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{X})=\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\bm{\xi}
that

\displaystyle\delta_{r}:=\sup_{\mathbf{X}\in D_{r}}\left|\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{X}\right)\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}^{2}\right|=\sup_{\mathbf{X}\in D_{r}}\left|\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{x}}\bm{\xi}\big\|_{2}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{x}}\bm{\xi}\big\|_{2}^{2}\right|.

(197)

Denote
\mathcal{B}:=\{\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}:\mathbf{X}\in D_{r}\}
.
We now estimate the parameters in Theorem
30
.
Note that it follows directly from the definition of
\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})
that
\big\|\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})\big\|_{2}=\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}=1

and hence
\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\big\|_{F}=\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}
for all
X\in\mathcal{S}^{d}
.
Thus, we have
d_{F}(\mathcal{B})=1
since
\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\|_{F}=\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}
for all
\mathbf{X}\in D_{r}
.
On the other hand,
for
\mathbf{X}\in D_{r}
,

\displaystyle m\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}^{T}=\textbf{Id}_{m},

(198)

which implies that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\big\|=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\big\|\text{vec}(\mathbf{X})\big\|_{2}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\big\|\mathbf{X}\big\|_{F}

(199)

and
d_{\|\cdot\|}(\mathcal{B})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}
.
From
(
Candès and Plan 2011
, Lemma 3.1)
,
it follows that the covering number for
d\times d
symmetric matrices with Frobenius norm
1
and rank at most
r
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(D_{r},\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F},\varepsilon)\leq\left(1+6/\varepsilon\right)^{(2d+1)r}.

(200)

Using Dudley’s integral estimate (see, e.g.,
(
Talagrand 2005
)
), combined with (
199
) and (
200
),
we obtain that

\displaystyle\gamma_{2}\left(\mathcal{B},\big\|\cdot\big\|\right)=\gamma_{2}\left(D_{r},\big\|\cdot\big\|_{F}\right)\leq C\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log(\mathcal{N}(D_{r},\|\cdot\|_{F},u))}du\leq C^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{dr}{m}}.

(201)

With the notations in Theorem
30
, we have

\displaystyle E=C^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{dr}{m}}\left(C^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{dr}{m}}+1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}},\quad V=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\left(C^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{dr}{m}}+1\right),\quad U=\frac{1}{m}.

(202)

Therefore, applying Theorem
30
,
we have
\delta_{r}\leq\delta
with probability at least
1-\varepsilon

when

m\geq C\delta^{-2}(rd+\log(2\varepsilon^{-1})).

Here,
C>0
denotes some universal constant.
This completes the proof of Lemma
6
.
∎

G.2
Proof of Lemma
8

Proof of Lemma
8
.

We will establish first that for all symmetric matrices
\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
with rank
\text{rank}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})=r
and
\text{rank}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})=r^{\prime}
it holds that

|\langle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z}_{1}),\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle|\leq\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}.

(203)

Let us remark that in the case of
\langle\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle=0
,
this inequality has been proven in
(
Candès and Plan 2011
, Lemma 3.3)
.
The following proof of this slightly more general statement is analogous.

To prove inequality (
203
) we assume without loss of generality
that
\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big\|_{F}=\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}=1
.
We note first that from the parallelogram identity, it follows that

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\right),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{2}\right)\rangle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{2}\right)\big\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\big\|\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2}\right)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}}{4}\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{1-\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}}{4}\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}}{2}\left(\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big\|_{F}^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}^{2}\right)+\langle\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle.

By rearranging terms and using the assumption
\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big\|_{F}=\big\|\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big\|_{F}=1

we obtain that

\langle(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{Z}_{1}),\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle=\langle\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\right),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathbf{Z}_{2}\right)\rangle-\langle\mathbf{Z}_{1},\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle\leq\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}.

Since the reverse bound

\displaystyle\langle(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{I})(\mathbf{Z}_{1}),\mathbf{Z}_{2}\rangle\geq-\delta_{r+r^{\prime}}

can be shown analogously, inequality (
203
) follows.

Next, we prove inequality (
10
).
For that, we note that
there exists a matrix
\mathbf{M}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r^{\prime}}
with
\big\|\mathbf{M}\big\|_{F}=1

such that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\mathbf{V}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\langle\left[\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\right]\mathbf{V},\mathbf{M}\rangle=\langle\left[\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\right],\mathbf{V}\mathbf{M}^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z}),\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{M}^{\top}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\rangle.

holds.
Using inequality (
203
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\mathbf{V}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{r+2r^{\prime}}\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|_{F}\big\|\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{V}\mathbf{M}^{\top}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{r+2r^{\prime}}\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathbf{V}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{M}\big\|_{F}=\delta_{r+2r^{\prime}}\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|_{F}.

This proves inequality (
10
).

Inequality (
11
) is a direct consequence of (
10
).
Indeed, let
\mathbf{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
\big\|\mathbf{v}\big\|_{2}=1
be an eigenvector
of

\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{Z}\right)

corresponding to the largest eigenvalue in absolute value.
It then follows from inequality (
10
) that

\big\|\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\big\|=\big\|\left[\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{Z})\right]\mathbf{v}\big\|_{2}\leq\delta_{r+2}\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|_{F}.

It remains to prove inequality (
14
).
Note that using the fact

\langle\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{Z})=0\rangle
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\mathcal{A}(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{A}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{Z})\right)\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle\left(\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{Z}))\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle\left(\mathcal{I}-\mathcal{A}^{*}\mathcal{A}\right)\left(\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\right),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{Z})\rangle|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\delta_{(r+1)+1}\big\|\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top}\big\|_{F}\big\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{w}^{\top},\bot}(\mathbf{Z})\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{r+2}\big\|\mathbf{Z}\big\|_{F},

where in inequality
(a)
we used (
203
).
This completes the proof of Lemma
8
.
∎
